              Chairman’s
              Statement
              董事長致辭
               To the Shareholders,
               On behalf of the board (the “Board”) of directors (the
               “Directors”) of Nanjing Sinolife United Company Limited* (the
               “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”), I
               am pleased to present the annual results of the Group for the
               year ended 31 December 2024 (the “Year”).


               致股東
               本 人 代 表 南 京 中 生 聯 合 股 份 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」，連
               同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）董事（「董事」）會（「董事
               會」）欣然提呈本集團截至 2024 年 12 月 31 日止年度（「本
               年度」）的全年業績。

              * For identification purpose only
              * 僅供識別

董事長致辭

CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT                                                                   董事長致辭
In 2024, the global economic growth slowed overall, with pronounced                     2024 年，全 球 經 濟 增 速 總 體 放 緩，區 域 分 化
regional divergence: developed economies experienced sluggish growth,                   顯 著，發 達 經 濟 體 增 長 疲 軟，新 興 市 場 韌 性
while emerging markets demonstrated resilience. As a key driver of global               較強。中國經濟作為全球經濟發展的引擎，
economic growth, China’s economic growth outpaced the global average,                   增速高於全球平均水平，消費升級與「新三樣」
with consumption upgrades and exports of the “New Trio” (electric vehicles,            （電 動 汽 車、鋰 電 池、光 伏）出 口 成 為 新 的 動
lithium-ion batteries and photovoltaic products) emerging as new growth                 力。主 要 經 濟 體 通 脹 降 溫 仍 然 存 在，貨 幣 政
drivers. While inflation cooled in major economies, it remained persistent,             策 轉 向 寬 鬆。地 緣 政 治 衝 突 持 續，俄 烏 衝 突
leading to a shift toward more accommodative monetary policies. Geopolitical            與巴以危機導致能源價格和貿易成本飆升，
conflicts continued, with the Russia-Ukraine conflict and the Israel-Palestine          中美關係緩和或為全球供應鏈調整帶來機遇，
crisis causing surges to energy prices and trade costs. Although a stabilisation        但「脫鈎」風險依舊存在。
in China-US relations may present opportunities for global supply chain
adjustments, the risk of “decoupling” remains.

In 2024, the Group successfully achieved its strategic objectives established          2024 年，總體達成年初本集團既定戰略目標。
at the beginning of the Year. It focused resources on developing cross-border          聚集資源發展跨境電子商務，優化電子商務
e-commerce, optimised promotional strategies on e-commerce platforms,                  平台的推廣策略，提升好健康品牌和關鍵產
and enhanced the influence of the Good Health brand and key products.                  品 的 影 響 力；繼 續 加 大 新 產 品 的 研 發，縮 短
The Group enhanced its research and development efforts for new products               研 發 周 期；加 強 供 應 鏈 管 理，提 升 產 能，縮
to shorten research and development cycles, strengthened supply chain                  短生產及採購周期，提升營運資金效率。
management, and expanded production capacity to reduce manufacturing
and procurement lead times, thereby enhancing working capital efficiency.

In 2025, geopolitical conflicts, debt crises, trade barriers, a restructuring global   2025 年，地 緣 政 治 衝 突、債 務 危 機、貿 易 壁
manufacturing landscape, and many other factors will continue to create                壘、全 球 製 造 業 格 局 調 整 等 因 素，使 得 全 球
uncertainties for global economic development. As the global economy                   經濟發展依舊充滿不確定性。全球經濟在通
continues to face policy competition and sluggish growth amid the cooling              脹降溫與貿易復甦中仍面臨政策博弈和增長
inflation and trade recovery, China is required to strike a balance between            乏力，中國經濟需在「內需重振」與「外部突圍」
“revitalising domestic demand” and “breaking through external barriers” for its        間 尋 找 平 衡。政 策 定 力、結 構 性 改 革、房 地
economic growth. Policy stability, structural reforms, mitigation of real estate       產與金融風險化解及國際合作將是應對多重
and financial risks, and international cooperation will be critical in addressing      挑戰的關鍵。中國的高質量發展若能有效激
these challenges. If China’s high-quality development can effectively drive            活消費與啟發創新，或為全球經濟注入新的
consumption and spark innovation, new momentum may be injected into the                動力。
global economy.

The Chinese consumer healthcare industry remains in a stage of high-quality            中國消費健康行業仍處於高質量、快速發展
and rapid development. China’s large population and their increasing health            的階段。中國龐大的人口基數和日益提升的
awareness form a solid foundation for the demand for healthcare products,              健康意識，構成了健康產品市場需求的堅實
offering huge advantages and opportunities. Omnichannel marketing                      基 礎，蘊 藏 著 巨 大 的 優 勢 和 機 遇。全 渠 道 營
strategies, including the extensive integration of online and offline platforms        銷策略則通過線上線下的廣泛融合和社交媒
and precise marketing on social media, effectively enhance consumer                    體的精準營銷，有效提升了消費者的購物體
shopping experiences and brand stickiness. The combination of online                   驗和品牌粘性。線上電商平台與線下實體店
e-commerce platforms and offline physical stores has expanded market                   的 結 合，擴 大 了 市 場 覆 蓋 面，而 社 交 媒 體 平
coverage, while interactions on social media platforms further enhance brand           台的互動則進一步增強了品牌知名度。跨境
recognition. In terms of cross-border products, due to a growing demand for            產品方面，隨著消費者對高品質健康產品的
high-quality healthcare products among consumers, imported healthcare                  需求增加，進口健康產品在市場上越來越受
products become increasingly popular in the market. As the development of              歡迎。跨境電商平台的發展為消費者提供了
cross-border e-commerce platforms provides convenient purchasing channels,             便捷的購買渠道，使海外品牌能夠更順利地

Management Discussion and Analysis
管理層討論及分析

Foreign exchange exposure                                                                 匯兌風險
The Group conducts in-bound transactions principally in RMB and outbound                  本集團境內業務主要以人民幣進行業務交易，
transactions principally in New Zealand dollar, United States dollar, and                 境外業務主要以新西蘭元、美元及澳元進行
Australian dollar. The Group managed its foreign exchange risks by performing             業務交易。本集團通過定期審查及監督其外
regular review and monitoring of its foreign exchange exposures. The Directors            匯敞口管理其外匯風險。董事認為本集團面
consider the Group’s risk exposure to currency fluctuation to be controlled.              臨 貨 幣 波 動 的 風 險 敞 口 可 控。因 此，本 集 團
Therefore, the Group had not implemented any hedging or other alternative                 於本年度並無實施任何對沖或其他替代政策
policies to deal with such exposure during the Year.                                      處理有關風險。

Committee with reference to the duties, responsibilities, performance of the              責任、表現及本集團業績而釐定及建議。
Directors and the results of the Group.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE CULTURE AND VALUE                                            企業管治文化及價值
The Company is committed to ensuring that its affairs are conducted in            本公司致力確保以崇高的商業道德標準營運
accordance with high business ethical standards. This reflects its belief that,   業務，反映其堅信如要達到長遠的業務目標，
in the achievement of its long-term business objectives, it is imperative to      必 須 以 誠 信、透 明 和 負 責 的 態 度 行 事。本 公
act with probity, transparency and accountability. By doing so, the Company       司相信這樣長遠可為股東取得最大的財富，
believes that Shareholder wealth will be maximised in the long term and that      而僱員、業務夥伴及公司營運業務的社區亦
its employees, those with whom it has business activity and the communities       可受惠。
in which it operates will all benefit.

Corporate governance is the process by which the Board instructs                  企業管治是董事局指導集團管理層如何營運
management of the Group to conduct its affairs with a view to ensuring that       業務以達成業務目標的過程。董事局致力維
its objectives are met. The Board is committed to maintaining and developing      持及建立完善的企業管治常規，以確保：
robust corporate governance practices that are intended to ensure:

•     satisfactory and sustainable returns to Shareholders;                       •   為股東帶來滿意及可持續的回報；

•     that the interests of those who deal with the Company are safeguarded;      •   保障本公司業務夥伴的利益；

•     that overall business risk is understood and managed appropriately;         •   了解並適當地管理整體業務風險；

•     the delivery of high-quality products and services to the satisfaction of   •   提供令客戶滿意的高質素產品與服務；
      customers; and                                                                  及

•     that high standards of business ethics are maintained.                      •   維持崇高的商業道德標準。

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                                    企業管治常規
The Board is committed to maintaining good corporate governance standards.        董事會致力於維持良好的企業管治標準。

The Board believes that good corporate governance standards are essential         董事會相信，良好的企業管治標準乃本公司
in providing a framework for the Company to safeguard the interests of            保 障 股 東 利 益、提 升 企 業 價 值、制 定 其 業 務
Shareholders, enhance corporate value, formulate its business strategies and      策略及政策以及提升其透明度及問責性之關鍵。
policies, and enhance its transparency and accountability.

(the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix C1 of the Listing Rules as the basis of     治守則（「企業管治守則」）之原則及守則條文

Board independence evaluation                                                           董事會獨立性評估
The Company has in place a mechanism that underpin an independent Board                 本公司於本年度已建立支持獨立董事會及其
and its views during the Year which sets out the processes and procedures to            意見的機制，當中所載程序旨在確保董事會
ensure a strong independent element on the Board, which allows the Board to             具備強大的獨立元素，使董事會能有效地行
effectively exercise independent judgment to better safeguard Shareholders’             使獨立判斷力，從而更好地保障股東權益。
interests.

The objectives of the evaluation are to improve Board effectiveness,                    評估的目的是提高董事會效率，最大限度發

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROLS                                               風險管理及內部監控
The Board acknowledges its responsibility for the risk management and               董事會承認其對本公司及其附屬公司的風險
internal control systems of the Company and its subsidiaries and reviewing          管理及內部監控制度以及每年審核其有效性
their effectiveness annually. Such systems are designed to manage rather than       的責任。該等制度旨在管理而非消除未能達
eliminate the risk of failure to achieve business objectives and can only provide   成業務目標的風險，而且只能就不會有重大
reasonable and not absolute assurance against material misstatement or loss.        的失實陳述或損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。

The Board has the responsibility for establishing, maintaining and evaluating       董事會負責制定、維持及評估有效的風險管
effective risk management and internal control systems. The board of                理及內部監控制度。監事會負責監督董事會
Supervisors is responsible for supervising the Board on the establishment           制定及實施風險管理及內部監控制度。管理
and implementation of risk management and internal control systems.                 層負責協助及監察風險管理及內部監控過程。
The management is responsible for coordinating and monitoring the                   審核委員會協助董事會有關內部監控的領導
risk management and internal control progress. The Audit Committee                  及 協 作 事 宜、監 督 設 計、實 施 及 監 察 風 險 管
assists the Board in leading and coordinating matters regarding internal            理及內部監控制度。
control, overseeing the design, implementation and monitoring of the risk
management and internal control systems.

The Audit Department of the Company is responsible for performing                   本公司的審核部門負責獨立檢討風險管理及
independent review of the adequacy and effectiveness of the risk management         內部監控制度的充足性及有效性。本公司審
and internal control systems. The Audit Department of the Company examines          核部門測試有關會計政策及所有重大控制的
key issues in relation to accounting practices and all material controls and        關鍵問題，及向審核委員會提供其發現。
provides its findings to the Audit Committee.

The objectives of risk management and internal control systems of the               本公司風險管理及內部監控制度的目標是確
Company are to ensure compliance with relevant laws and regulations,                保 遵 守 相 關 法 律 法 規、保 障 本 公 司 資 產、保
safeguard the Company’s assets, maintain proper accounting records and              持完整會計記錄及提高營運效率，以實現發
improve operational efficiency, so as to achieve strategic development.             展戰略。

functions and areas, namely organizational structure, strategic development,        功 能 及 領 域，例 如 組 織 架 構、發 展 戰 略、人
human resources, asset management, sales and purchase, finance and                  力 資 源、資 產 管 理、銷 售 及 採 購、財 務 及 會
accounting, information technology, internal audit, contract management and         計、資 訊 科 技、內 部 審 計、合 同 管 理 及 行 政
administration.                                                                     管理。

procedures and guidelines with defined authority for implementation by key          有界定實施權限的若干風險管理程序及指引。
business processes. The control activities are mainly carried out through the       控制活動主要透過以下各項進行：

DEVELOPMENT POTENTIAL: EXERCISE POWER AND                                                  發展力 · 讓權責共履
FULFIL RESPONSIBILITIES IN UNISON
The Group has always regarded compliance as the cornerstone of ESG                         本集團始終將合規視為 ESG 管治的基石，以「權
governance, and continues to optimise and improve our ESG governance                       責共履」為核心準則，持續優化和完善 ESG 管
framework under the core principle of “exercising power and fulfilling                     治架構。通過將可持續發展理念深度融入戰
responsibilities in unison”. By deeply integrating sustainable development                 略決策，並依托完善的 ESG 管治架構與高效的
concepts into strategic decision-making, as well as capitalizing on a                      日常管理，我們在保障業務穩健增長的同時，
comprehensive ESG governance framework and efficient day-to-day                            積極履行社會責任，創造積極的社會影響力，
management, we ensure robust business growth while actively fulfilling our                 推動企業發展與社會、環境責任的協同共進。
social responsibilities, as part of our commitment to creating positive social             我們攜手利益相關方共同創造長期價值，為
impacts, as well as promoting the synergy between corporate development                    可持續發展目標的實現提供堅實保障。
and social and environmental responsibilities and their advancement. Working
in unison with our stakeholders, we co-create long-term values and provide a
solid foundation for achieving sustainable development goals.

ESG Governance                                                                             ESG 管治
The Group actively practices the concept of sustainable development by                     本集團積極踐行可持續發展理念，持續完善
continuing to improve the ESG governance structure and management                          ESG 管治架構及管理體系，持續推進可持續發
system, while advancing the integration of sustainable development with                    展理念與戰略發展與經營決策相融合。在保
strategic development and operational decisions. While ensuring stable                     障經營業績穩健增長的同時，我們積極履行
growth in our business performance, we actively fulfil our social responsibilities         社 會 責 任，創 造 良 好 的 社 會 影 響 力，努 力 攜
to create positive social impacts, and strive to move forward with various                 手各利益相關方通向可持續發展之路。
stakeholders towards sustainable development.

Governance Structure                                                                       管治架構
Our ESG governance framework has been established and improved to clarify                  建 立 健 全 ESG 管 治 架 構，明 確 各 層 級 職 責 分
the division of responsibilities at all levels, which has in return enhanced ESG           工，為強化 ESG 管治能力和確保 ESG 管理成效
governance capabilities and given effective safeguards for the effective ESG               提供了有力保障。我們建立了由董事會、ESG
management. We have established a three-tier ESG governance structure                      管理委員會和 ESG 工作小組構成的三級 ESG 管
composed of the Board, the ESG Management Committee, and the ESG Task                      治架構，以董事會作為最高決策層，通過 ESG
Force, with the Board as the highest decision-making authority to implement                管理委員會實施 ESG 相關工作的統籌管理。
the unified management of ESG-related work through the ESG Management
Committee.

Board Statement                                                                            董事會聲明
Overall Responsibility Borne by the Board                                                  董事會肩負總體責任
The Board assumes overall supervision, guidance and review responsibilities                董事會對本集團的 ESG 工作承擔整體監察、指
for the Group’s ESG initiatives. The Board takes responsibility for establishing           導及檢討責任。董事會負責制定本集團的 ESG

targets and their progress, monitoring the ESG performance, and approving                  標 及 達 成 情 況、監 督 ESG 表 現，並 審 批 年 度
the annual ESG report. The Board authorizes the ESG Management Committee                   ESG 報告。董事會授權 ESG 管理委員會協助監
to assist in monitoring the implementation of the ESG strategy, the execution              督 ESG 策略實施、執行 ESG 風險管理與內部監
of ESG risk management and internal control, and other work.                               控等工作。

potential product quality and safety incidents.

                                           Sinolife United’s Supplier Engagement Activities
                                                     中生聯合供應商交流活動

 Supplier Engagement Case Study
 供應商交流案例
 Based on product needs and development strategies, Sinolife United collaborated with suppliers to explore raw material selection
 and compatibility, and assisted suppliers in identifying innovative materials better suited to product requirements, so as to enhance
 product performance and quality.
 中生聯合結合產品需求和發展戰略，與供應商共同探討原料的選型和匹配問題，協助供應商選擇更適合產品要求的
 新型原料，提高產品的性能和質量。

 Through optimized raw material selection, Sinolife United significantly reduced the production cost of its Garcinia Capsules product
 while also shortening the delivery cycle. Meanwhile, the Company has established a closer partnership with its suppliers, improving
 supplier satisfaction and enhancing the stability and reliability of the supply chain.
 中生聯合通過優化原料選型，使本公司「藤黃果」產品成本大幅降低，也縮短了交貨周期。同時，本公司與供應商之
 間建立了更加緊密的合作關係，提高了供應商的滿意度，增強了供應鏈的穩定性和可靠性。

Water Resource Management                                                          水資源管理
Effective water resource management is crucial to both our business                有效的水資源管理對企業運營和生態環境的
operations and ecological sustainability. The water we use is mainly sourced       可持續發展至關重要。我們的用水主要來源
from municipal water supply and rainwater reuse, with consumption mainly           於市政用水與雨水回用，水資源消耗主要集
concentrated in daily operation of the office premises and production in the       中在園區日常運營辦公與生產。本集團遵守
industrial park. The Group complies with various laws and regulations in the      《中華人民共和國水法》、新西蘭《水務法案，
place where we operate, such as the Water Law of People’s Republic of China        2021》等運營所在地法律法規。各子公司依據
and the New Zealand Water Services Act, 2021. All of our subsidiaries have         自身業務運營實際情況，均建立相應的用水
established water management standard operating procedures tailored to             管理標準操作程序，以規範生產運營用水管
their respective business operations to regulate water usage in production and     理。報 告 期 內，我 們 未 有 發 生 任 何 與 用 水 安
operations. During the Reporting Period, no water safety-related issues were       全有關的問題。
recorded.

We implement comprehensive and refined water resource management by                我們全面推進水資源精細化管理，通過持續
continuously monitoring consumption, upgrading water supply networks,              監 測、管 網 改 造、節 水 技 術 改 造、推 廣 使 用
adopting water-saving technologies, promoting the use of water-efficient           節 水 器 具，以 及 引 入 雨 水 回 用 系 統 等，提 升
appliances, and integrating rainwater recycle systems, so as to enhance water      用水效率，實現水資源循環利用。
efficiency and achieve water resource recycling.

 Smart Sprinkler Irrigation System and Rainwater Recycle Project
 智能噴灌系統與雨水回用項目
 In 2024, Sinolife United upgraded our original manual watering system to an automated timed sprinkler system. By integrating
 automation control with rainwater recycling technology, the project has effectively reduced water consumption and enhanced
 water management efficiency. Simultaneously, a 10-tonne rainwater storage tank was constructed and integrated into the irrigation
 network to enable rainwater recycling, thereby reducing the environmental impact of traditional irrigation practices.
 2024 年，中生聯合將原有人工澆水方式改造為自動定時噴淋系統，通過自動化控制與雨水回收技術的結合，有效降
 低水資源消耗，提升用水管理效能，並同步建設 10 噸容量雨水蓄水池集成於灌溉管網，實現雨水資源循環利用，減
 少傳統灌溉模式的環境負荷。


 Rainwater Drainage System Upgrade and Renovation Project
 雨水管網系統升級改造項目
 To address the risks of water accumulation and water wastage caused by the natural runoff drainage model in older factory
 areas, Sinolife United implemented a systematic upgrade and renovation of the rainwater drainage system in 2024 targeting key
 operational areas such as roads, green belts and parking lots at our operational sites. The project replaced the original natural
 runoff approach with a structured rainwater collection system. By scientifically deploying rainwater diversion pipelines, the project
 effectively mitigated water accumulation issues while enabling the standardized collection and reuse of rainwater, thereby
 reducing water consumption intensity.
 為解決老廠區原有雨水自然徑流排放模式存在的積水隱患及水資源浪費問題，2024 年中生聯合對運營地道路、綠化帶、
 停車場等關鍵區域實施系統性雨水管網改造，構建定向雨水收集系統，替代原有自然散水方式。通過雨水導流管網
 的科學佈設，有效緩了解廠區積水問題，同時實現雨水資源規範化收集與再利用，降低水資源消耗強度。

 Establishment of Sustainable Packaging Management System by Living Nature
 Living Nature 構建可持續包裝管理體系
 During the Reporting Period, Living Nature implemented a sustainable packaging approach by developing a fully recyclable
 packaging solution across the entire supply chain. Both product inner bottles and outer boxes are made from 100% recyclable
 materials, while a priority recycling mechanism has been adopted for supplier-delivered cardboard to ensure full lifecycle
 environmental impact management of packaging materials.

 材料，並對供應商到貨紙板實施優先循環利用機制，實現包裝材料全生命周期環境負荷管控。

Response to Climate Change                                                                 應對氣候變化
Climate change poses a severe threat to human well-being and global                        氣候變化對人類福祉以及地球生態平衡構成
ecological balance. The increasing frequency of extreme weather events and                 了嚴重的威脅，極端天氣與自然災害的頻繁
natural disasters presents multiple challenges to societal security, ecosystem             為人類社會的安全與生態系統的穩定性、經
stability and the continuity of economic activities. It is therefore imperative            濟活動連續性構成多重挑戰，亟需通過系統
to strengthen climate resilience and accelerate emissions reduction process                性行動加強氣候適應能力建設並推進減排進
through systematic actions. The Group implements green energy development                  程。本集團實施綠色能源發展及替代利用、
and substitution, deploys high-efficiency energy-saving and carbon-reduction               高效節能降碳技術及設備應用，構建綠色低
technologies and equipment, and establishes a green and low-carbon                         碳產業鏈，大力推廣低碳零碳負碳技術的創
industrial chain. We actively drive the innovation and application of low-                 新應用，不斷構築可持續發展的綠色生態圈。
carbon, zero-carbon and negative-carbon technologies, continuously building
a green ecosystem for sustainable development.

opportunities, continuously improving internal governance mechanisms and                   遇，不 斷 完 善 內 部 管 理 機 制 和 方 法，持 續 提
methodologies to continuously enhance the effectiveness of climate change                  高 氣 候 變 化 治 理 的 有 效 性，形 成 董 事 會 —

comprising the Board — ESG Management Committee — ESG Task Force.                          治理架構。董事會負責制定減碳目標及戰略，

The ESG Management Committee is responsible for regularly reviewing and                    化 相 關 事 宜、目 標 等 的 執 行 情 況，對 已 識 別

response strategy and reporting progress to its superiors on a regular basis.

and management. To optimize resource allocation and enhance the efficiency                環 節。為 優 化 資 源 配 置，提 高 氣 候 變 化 風 險
of climate change risk management, we have integrated the climate change                  管理效率，我們將氣候變化風險管理流程整

assess the identified climate change risks, and introduce effective management            的氣候變化風險開展積極的評估並制定有效

a lower-carbon and more sustainable business model. In 2024, we formulated         可 持 續 的 商 業 模 式 轉 型。2024 年，我 們 制 定

Energy Management                                                                  能源管理
We are committed to improving energy use efficiency as a core initiative           提升能源使用效能是我們踐行低碳發展戰略
in implementing our low-carbon development strategy. By systematically             的核心舉措。通過系統性構建能源管理體系、
establishing an energy management system, deepening energy-saving                  深化生產工藝節能優化及完善能效動態監測
optimization in production processes, and enhancing the dynamic monitoring         機制，我們著力推進全價值鏈能效升級。
mechanisms for energy efficiency, we are dedicated to driving energy
efficiency upgrading across the entire value chain.

The Group complies with the relevant energy management laws and                    本集團遵循《中華人民共和國能源節約法》等
regulations of places in which we operate, such as the Law of the People’s         各運營地能源管理相關法律法規，各運營地
Republic of China on Energy Conservation , and each of our operational sites       結合實際情況制定相應的能源管理設施標準
has introduced corresponding standard operating procedures (SOPs) for              操作程序 (SOPs)，高效地推進各項節能減排工
energy management facilities in light of their respective actual conditions,       作的實施。我們致力於在研發和生產過程、
efficiently facilitating the implementation of various energy-saving and           工藝優化和運營等各階段運用創新的理念開
emission-reduction measures. We are committed to applying innovative               展技術優化，從而提前規避非必要的能源消耗。
concepts in various stages, including R&D, production processes, process
optimization, and operations, to achieve technological optimization, thereby
avoiding unnecessary energy consumption in advance.

 Energy-Saving Equipment Application
 節能設備應用
 •     Electric-powered ride-on mowers and sweepers were purchased to reduce energy consumption of equipment within the
       park.
 •     通過採購電力驅動的坐騎式割草機和掃地機，減少園區內設備的能源消耗。

 •     During the park road renovation, we replaced all streetlights with solar-powered streetlights, effectively enhancing energy
       use efficiency.
 •     在園區道路改造中，我們將所有路燈更換為太陽能路燈，有效提升能源使用效率。


Green Office                                                                            綠色辦公
By promoting green office concepts and fostering a low-carbon consciousness             通過倡導綠色辦公理念與培養全員低碳意識，
across the Company, we integrate energy-saving and emission-reduction                   我們將節能減排實踐延伸至日常運營細節，
practices in every aspect of daily operations. This approach forms a low-               形成技術革新與行為改善協同驅動的低碳轉
carbon transition path driven by technological innovation and behavioral                型路徑，為應對氣候變化和實現低碳目標提
improvement, providing robust support for combating climate change and                  供有力支撐。
achieving low-carbon targets.

We advocate for green and low-carbon office practices, by issuing a “Green              我們倡導綠色低碳的辦公方式，發佈「綠色倡
Initiative” and establishing environmental office “5R” principles.                      議書」，制定環保辦公「5R」原則。

set climate targets and monitors the progress of greenhouse gas emission and            候目標並監督溫室氣體減排和能源降耗工作
energy consumption reduction efforts. These initiatives drive our transition            的進展情況，推動企業向更低碳和更可持續
toward a lower-carbon and more sustainable business model. During the                   的 商 業 模 式 轉 型。報 告 期 內，本 集 團 溫 室 氣
reporting period, the Group’s greenhouse gas emission intensity was 16.65 kg            體 排 放 強 度 為 16.65 千 克 二 氧 化 碳 當 量╱萬

 Employee Recruitment Highlights
 員工招聘亮點工作
 We encourage employees to share job postings through their social network, so that their network resources are fully utilized
 to increase the visibility and recruitment opportunities for positions. Based on the specific features of each job, we implement
 targeted push notification strategies that deliver recruitment information to specific groups, further enhancing the recruitment
 efficiency.
 我們鼓勵員工通過朋友圈分享招聘崗位信息，充分利用人脉資源提升崗位曝光率和招聘機會。針對不同崗位特點，
 我們實施精準推送策略，向特定人群定向展示招聘信息，以進一步提高招聘效率。

 The social networking, as an efficient channel for disseminating information, facilitates the rapid expansion of the pool of potential
 candidates, while effectively reducing the costs associated with traditional recruitment channels. Furthermore, by having
 employees share job information, we showcase the Company’s culture and values, attracting like-minded talent and further
 enhancing our brand image.
 朋友圈作為信息傳播的高效渠道，能夠快速擴大潛在候選人範圍，同時有效降低傳統招聘渠道的成本。此外，通過
 員工分享崗位信息，我們得以展示公司文化與價值觀，吸引志同道合的人才，並進一步提升公司品牌形象。

 personal growth.

 engagement activities in 2024. These culture-themed events not only highlighted the appreciation and empathy of expatriate
 employees towards different cultural backgrounds but also promoted cross-departmental collaboration and communication,

 2024 Remuneration and Performance Management Highlights
 2024 年薪酬績效管理亮點工作
 •    For the remuneration and performance assessment in the e-commerce department, a more flexible commission system and
      a new performance review and approval process were introduced. Furthermore, the successful transition to a paperless office
      system ensures an expeditious and efficient approval process while facilitating real-time access to employee information,
      thereby improving overall efficiency;
 •    電商部門在薪酬績效考核中引入更為靈活的提成制以及新的績效考核審批流程，實現了流程的無紙化辦公轉型，
      使審批過程更加迅速和高效，同時也便於隨時檢索員工信息，提高工作效率；

 •    We continue to optimize the performance schemes and included new target management measures. Meanwhile, an incentive
      mechanism for releasing viral articles was introduced for new media roles, while the performance assessment standards for
      e-commerce management positions were further refined. Furthermore, personalized incentive schemes were introduced and
      implemented for the live-streaming department;
 •    持續優化績效方案並增設目標管理，為新媒體崗位引入爆文激勵機制，細化電商管理崗位績效考核標準，同
      時在主播部門增設並實施了個性化激勵方案；

 •    Allowance packages introduced for e-commerce positions significantly galvanized employees’ enthusiasm and motivation.
 •    增設電商崗位津貼，極大地激發了員工的工作熱情和積極性。

                                通過適崗培訓，員工能夠更快地融入團隊，為公司的發展貢獻力量。

業務培訓                            of different roles, we have designed a series of highly practical and professional courses,

                                業素養和工作效率。

146                                                        N A N J I N G S I N O L I FE U N I TE D C O M PA N Y L I M I TE D   A N N U A L RE POR T 2024
Environmental, Social and Governance Report
環境、社會及管治報告

 Safety Training and Emergency Drills
 安全培訓與應急演練
Sinolife United
Sinolife United’s safety training session for all employees in December 2024 covered various aspects such as safety operating
procedures, emergency drill exercises, and the identification of “missing persons” clues, which was aimed at enhancing employees’
safety awareness and operational skills, effectively improving emergency response speed and team collaboration capabilities.

中生聯合
2024 年 12 月，中生聯合組織開展覆蓋全體員工的安全培訓，涵蓋安全操作規程、應急預案演練、尋找「失蹤人員」線
索等多個方面，旨在提高員工安全意識、安全操作技能，有效增強應急響應速度和團隊協作能力。




Living Nature
During the reporting period, Living Nature organized a series of training sessions and emergency drills to raise employees’ safety
consciousness in operating procedures. For instance Living Nature focused on providing specialized training tailored to laboratory
staff on the storage and safe handling of 70% ethanol, encompassing processes such as dispensing, distribution, and storage. This
ensured the proper storage of ethanol and eliminated potential safety hazards. Through systematic training and drills, the Company
further reinforced employees’ adherence to safety protocols and enhanced the safety management standards of the laboratory.

Living Nature
報告期內，Living Nature 為提升員工的安全操作意識，組織開展系列培訓及應急演練活動。針對實驗室員工，Living
Nature 重點開展 70% 乙醇存儲及安全操作培訓，涵蓋分裝、分配和儲存等環節，確保乙醇的妥善存放，杜絕安全隱患。
通過系統化的培訓和演練，進一步強化員工的安全操作規範，提升實驗室安全管理水平。

Good Health
During the reporting period, Good Health organized special occupational health and safety training activities. As for forklift operators
that require license renewal, we conducted specific training sessions on forklift safety operations, hazard identification, and
compliance requirements so that all operators fulfil safety standards. Such arrangement reduced the risk of workplace accidents.

 Meanwhile, Good Health provided recurrent training for all first-aid personnel, covering the latest first aid procedures, CPR, wound
 management, and emergency response techniques. This training aimed to enhance on-site emergency preparedness, ensuring
 that employees receive timely and effective care in emergency situations.

好健康
報告期內，好健康組織開展專項的職業健康與安全培訓活動。針對需要更新執照的叉車操作員，開展了叉車安全操
作、危險識別及合規要求專項培訓，確保所有操作員符合安全標準，減少工作場所事故風險。

 同時，針對所有急救員，好健康開展了包括最新急救程序、CPR、傷口處理及應急響應技術的急救複訓培訓，提高
 現場應急準備能力，確保員工在緊急情況下獲得及時有效的護理。

OPINION                                                                           意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Nanjing Sinolife         我們已審計第 164 至 258 頁所載南京中生聯合
United Company Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”)         股份有限公司（「貴公司」）及其附屬公司（「貴

profit or loss and other comprehensive income, the consolidated statement of      綜合損益及其他全面收益表、綜合權益變動
changes in equity and the consolidated statement of cash flows for the year       表及綜合現金流量表以及綜合財務報表附註，
then ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including         包括重大會計政策資料。
material accounting policy information.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and fair view   我們認為，綜合財務報表根據香港會計師公
of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31 December 2024,       會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈之香港財務報告
and of its consolidated financial performance and its consolidated cash flows     準則（「香港財務報告準則」）真實及公平地反
for the year then ended in accordance with Hong Kong Financial Reporting          映了 貴集團於 2024 年 12 月 31 日之綜合財務
Standards (“HKFRSs”) issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public        狀況及截至該日止年度之綜合財務表現及其
Accountants (“HKICPA”) and have been properly prepared in compliance with         綜合現金流量，並已根據香港公司條例之披
the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.                 露規定妥善編製。

     Standards (“HKASs”) and Interpretations) issued by the Hong Kong                                  所有香港財務報告準則、香港會計準則
     Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”), and the disclosure                         （「香港會計準則」）及詮釋）以及香港公

2.1 BASIS OF PREPARATION (CONTINUED)                                               2.1 編製基準（續）
    Basis of consolidation                                                           合併基準
    The consolidated financial statements include the financial statements of       綜合財務報表包括本公司及其附屬公司
    the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”)      （統 稱 為「本 集 團」）於 截 至 2024 年 12 月
    for the year ended 31 December 2024. A subsidiary is an entity (including       31 日止年度之財務報表。附屬公司乃本
    a structured entity), directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company.        公司直接或間接控制的實體（包括結構
    Control is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to variable       性 實 體）。當 本 集 團 透 過 參 與 被 投 資 方
    returns from its involvement with the investee and has the ability to           而享有或有權獲得可變回報，且有能力
    affect those returns through its power over the investee (i.e., existing        透過對被投資方的權力影響該等回報（即
    rights that give the Group the current ability to direct the relevant           現有權利賦予本集團目前指示被投資方
    activities of the investee).                                                    相關活動的能力）時，即屬於擁有控制權。

    Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting rights results       於一般情況下均存在多數投票權形成控
    in control. When the Company has less than a majority of the voting              制權之推定。當本公司擁有的被投資方
    or similar rights of an investee, the Group considers all relevant facts         投票權或類似權利不及大半，則評估是
    and circumstances in assessing whether it has power over an investee,            否擁有對被投資方的權力時，本集團會
    including:                                                                       考慮所有相關事實及情況，包括：

    (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote holders of the             (a)   與被投資方的其他投票持有者之
          investee;                                                                        間的合同安排；

    (b)   rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and                    (b)   從其他合同安排取得的權力；及

    (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.                     (c)   本集團的投票權及潛在投票權。

    The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared for the same           附屬公司財務報表乃按與本公司一致的
    reporting period as the Company, using consistent accounting policies.           報告期及會計政策編製。附屬公司業績
    The results of subsidiaries are consolidated from the date on which the          的合併始於本集團獲得對該等附屬公司
    Group obtains control, and continue to be consolidated until the date            的控制權之時，並止於本集團喪失有關
    that such control ceases.                                                        控制權之時。

    equity transaction.                                                                          以股本交易入賬。

    If the Group loses control over a subsidiary, it derecognises the related                    倘本集團失去對附屬公司的控制權，則

    the exchange fluctuation reserve; and recognises the fair value of any                       任何非控股權益及匯兌波動儲備；並確
    investment retained and any resulting surplus or deficit in profit or                        認所保留任何投資的公允值及損益賬中
    loss. The Group’s share of components previously recognised in other                         任何因此產生的盈餘或虧絀。先前已於

    as appropriate, on the same basis as would be required if the Group had                      重新分類至損益或保留溢利（如適用），

2.2 CHANGES IN ACCOUNTING POLICIES AND                                              2.2 會計政策及披露變動（續）
    DISCLOSURES (CONTINUED)
    The nature and the impact of the revised HKFRSs are described below:              經修訂香港財務報告準則的性質及影響
                                                                                      載述如下：

    (a)   Amendments to HKFRS 16 specify the requirements that a                      (a)   香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 16 號 修 訂 本
          seller-lessee uses in measuring the lease liability arising in a                  訂明賣方 — 承租人於計量售後回
          sale and leaseback transaction to ensure the seller-lessee does                   租交易中產生的租賃負債時所採

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                    2.4 重大會計政策
    Business combinations and goodwill                                                業務合併及商譽
    Business combinations are accounted for using the acquisition method.             業務合併乃以收購法列賬。已轉讓對價
    The consideration transferred is measured at the acquisition date fair            乃以收購日期的公允值計量，該公允值
    value which is the sum of the acquisition date fair values of assets              為本集團轉讓的資產於收購日期的公允
    transferred by the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group to the former          值、本集團向被收購方前擁有人承擔的
    owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued by the Group in            負債，及本集團發行以換取被收購方控
    exchange for control of the acquiree. For each business combination,              制權的股本權益的總和。於各業務合併
    the Group elects whether to measure the non-controlling interests in the          中，本集團選擇是否以公允值或被於收
    acquiree that are present ownership interests and entitle their holders to        購方可識別淨資產的應佔比例，計量於
    a proportionate share of net assets in the event of liquidation at fair value     被收購方的非控股權益，即賦予持有人
    or at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable net assets.          在清盤時按比例分佔淨資產的現有所有
    All other components of non-controlling interests are measured at fair            權權益。非控股權益的所有其他組成部
    value. Acquisition-related costs are expensed as incurred.                        分均按公允值計量。收購相關成本於產
                                                                                      生時列為開支。

    The Group determines that it has acquired a business when the acquired            當所收購的一組活動及資產包括一項投
    set of activities and assets includes an input and a substantive process          入及一個實質性過程而兩者對創造產出
    that together significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs.          之能力有重大貢獻時，本集團確定其已
                                                                                      收購一項業務。

    When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the financial assets              當本集團收購一項業務時，會根據合同
    and liabilities assumed for appropriate classification and designation            條款、於收購日期的經濟環境及相關條
    in accordance with the contractual terms, economic circumstances                  件，評估須承擔的金融資產及負債，以
    and pertinent conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the            作出適合的分類及標示，其中包括分離
    separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the acquiree.             被收購方主合同中的嵌入式衍生工具。

    If the business combination is achieved in stages, the previously held            如業務合併分階段進行，先前持有的股
    equity interest is remeasured at its acquisition date fair value and any          本權益按其於收購日期的公允值重新計
    resulting gain or loss is recognised in profit or loss.                           量，所產生的任何收益或虧損在損益中
                                                                                      確認。

    is classified as equity is not remeasured and subsequent settlement is                          不重新計量，其後結算在權益中入賬。
    accounted for within equity.

    Goodwill is initially measured at cost, being the excess of the aggregate                       商譽最初按成本計量，即已轉讓對價、
    of the consideration transferred, the amount recognised for non-                                非控股權益的確認金額及本集團先前持

    liabilities assumed. If the sum of this consideration and other items is                        之間的差額。如對價與其他項目的總額

    reassessment, recognised in profit or loss as a gain on bargain purchase.                       估後該差額於損益內確認為議價收購之
                                                                                                    收益。

    After initial recognition, goodwill is measured at cost less any                                於初始確認後，商譽按成本減任何累計
    accumulated impairment losses. Goodwill is tested for impairment                                減 值 虧 損 計 量。商 譽 須 每 年 作 減 值 測
    annually or more frequently if events or changes in circumstances                               試，若有事件發生或情況改變顯示賬面
    indicate that the carrying value may be impaired. The Group performs its                        值有可能減值時，則會更頻密地進行測
    annual impairment test of goodwill as at 31 December. For the purpose                           試。本集團於 12 月 31 日進行商譽的年度
    of impairment testing, goodwill acquired in a business combination                              減值測試。為進行減值測試，因業務合
    is, from the acquisition date, allocated to each of the Group’s cash-                           併而購入的商譽自收購日期起被分配至
    generating units, or groups of cash-generating units, that are expected                         預期可從合併產生的協同效益中獲益的
    to benefit from the synergies of the combination, irrespective of whether                       本集團各現金產生單位或現金產生單位
    other assets or liabilities of the Group are assigned to those units or                         組別，而無論本集團其他資產或負債是
    groups of units.                                                                                否已分配至該等單位或單位組別。

    Impairment is determined by assessing the recoverable amount of the                             減值乃通過評估與商譽有關的現金產生
    cash-generating unit (group of cash-generating units) to which the                              單位（或現金產生單位組別）的可收回金
    goodwill relates. Where the recoverable amount of the cash-generating                           額 釐 定。當 現 金 產 生 單 位（或 現 金 產 生
    unit (group of cash-generating units) is less than the carrying amount,                         單位組別）的可收回金額低於賬面金額
    an impairment loss is recognised. An impairment loss recognised for                             時，確認減值虧損。已就商譽確認的減
    goodwill is not reversed in a subsequent period.                                                值虧損不得於隨後期間撥回。

    Where goodwill has been allocated to a cash-generating unit (or group                           如商譽分配至現金產生單位（或現金產
    of cash-generating units) and part of the operation within that unit is                         生單位組別）而該單位的部分業務已出
    disposed of, the goodwill associated with the operation disposed of is                          售，則在釐定出售收益或虧損時，與所
    included in the carrying amount of the operation when determining the                           出售業務相關的商譽會計入該業務的賬
    gain or loss on the disposal. Goodwill disposed of in these circumstances                       面金額。在該等情況下出售的商譽乃根

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                                       2.4 重大會計政策（續）
    Fair value measurement                                                           公允值計量
    Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or paid to       公允值指於計量日期之市場參與者之間
    transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market participants       的有序交易中，就出售資產所收取之價
    at the measurement date. The fair value measurement is based on the              格或轉讓債務所支付之價格。公允值計
    presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer the liability     量乃基於假設出售資產或轉讓債務之交
    takes place either in the principal market for the asset or liability, or in     易於資產或負債之主要市場，或在未有
    the absence of a principal market, in the most advantageous market               主要市場之情況下，則於資產或負債之
    for the asset or liability. The principal or the most advantageous market        最有利市場進行。主要或最有利市場須
    must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability       位於本集團能到達之地方。資產或負債
    is measured using the assumptions that market participants would use             之公允值乃使用市場參與者為資產或負
    when pricing the asset or liability, assuming that market participants act       債定價所用之假設計量（假設市場參與
    in their economic best interest.                                                 者依照彼等之最佳經濟利益行事）。

    A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account             非金融資產之公允值計量乃經計及一名
    a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using            市場參與者透過使用其資產之最高及最
    the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market         佳用途或透過將資產出售予將使用其最
    participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.                高及最佳用途之另一名市場參與者而能
                                                                                     夠產生經濟利益的能力。

    The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in the                  本集團使用適用於不同情況之估值技
    circumstances and for which sufficient data are available to measure fair        術，而其有足夠數據計量公允值，以盡
    value, maximising the use of relevant observable inputs and minimising           量利用相關可觀察輸入數據及盡量減少
    the use of unobservable inputs.                                                  使用不可觀察輸入數據。

    All assets and liabilities for which fair value is measured or disclosed in      於財務報表計量或披露公允值之所有資
    the financial statements are categorised within the fair value hierarchy,        產及負債，均根據對公允值計量整體而
    described as follows, based on the lowest level input that is significant to     言屬重要之最低層輸入數據在下述公允
    the fair value measurement as a whole:                                           值等級架構內進行分類：

    Level 1     —     based on quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets          第一層 — 按同等資產或負債於活躍市
                      for identical assets or liabilities                                  場之報價（未經調整）計算

    Level 2     —     based on valuation techniques for which the                    第二層 — 按 估 值 技 術 計 算（藉 此 直 接
                      lowest level input that is significant to the fair value             或間接可觀察且對公允值計
                      measurement is observable, either directly or indirectly             量而言屬重要之最低層輸入
                                                                                           數據）

    Level 3     —     based on valuation techniques for which the                    第三層 — 按 估 值 技 術 計 算（藉 此 不 可
                      lowest level input that is significant to the fair value             觀察且對公允值計量而言屬
                      measurement is unobservable                                          重要之最低層輸入數據）

    For assets and liabilities that are recognised in the financial statements       就按經常性基準於財務報表確認之資產
    on a recurring basis, the Group determines whether transfers have                及負債而言，本集團於各報告期末通過
    occurred between levels in the hierarchy by reassessing categorisation           重新評估分類（基於對公允值計量整體
    (based on the lowest level input that is significant to the fair value           而言屬重大之最低層輸入數據）以決定
    measurement as a whole) at the end of each reporting period.                     等級架構內各層之間是否有轉移。

    Impairment of non-financial assets                                                           非金融資產之減值
    Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual impairment                          倘有跡象顯示出現減值或須就資產（存

    recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable amount is                            則會估計資產之可收回金額。資產之可
    the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s value in use and                         收回金額為資產或現金產生單位之在用
    its fair value less costs of disposal, and is determined for an individual                   價值及公允值減出售成本（以較高者為
    asset, unless the asset does not generate cash inflows that are largely                      準），並 就 個 別 資 產 釐 定，除 非 有 關 資
    independent of those from other assets or groups of assets, in which                         產並無產生在頗大程度上獨立於其他
    case the recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating                            資產或資產組別之現金流入。在此情況
    unit to which the asset belongs. In testing a cash-generating unit for                       下，可收回金額乃就資產所屬現金產生
    impairment, a portion of the carrying amount of a corporate asset (e.g., a                   單位釐定。於對現金產生單位進行減值
    headquarters building) is allocated to an individual cash-generating unit                    測試時，倘公司資產（例如總部樓宇）賬
    if it can be allocated on a reasonable and consistent basis or, otherwise,                   面價值的一部分可按合理及一致基礎予
    to the smallest group of cash-generating units.                                              以分配，則分配至單個現金產生單位，
                                                                                                 否則將分配至最小現金產生單位組別。

    An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount of an asset                     減值虧損僅於資產賬面值超逾其可收回
    exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing value in use, the estimated                     金額時確認。於評估在用價值時，估計
    future cash flows are discounted to their present value using a pre-                         日後現金流量按可反映貨幣時間價值的
    tax discount rate that reflects current market assessments of the time                       現時市場評估及資產特定風險之稅前貼
    value of money and the risks specific to the asset. An impairment loss is                    現率貼現至其現值。減值虧損於產生期
    charged to profit or loss in the period in which it arises in those expense                  間自損益中與減值資產功能相一致之開
    categories consistent with the function of the impaired asset.                               支類別中扣除。

    An assessment is made at the end of each reporting period as to                              資產乃於各報告期末進行評估，以確定
    whether there is an indication that previously recognised impairment                         是否有跡象顯示之前已確認之減值虧損
    losses may no longer exist or may have decreased. If such an indication                      不再存在或可能已經減少。倘出現該等
    exists, the recoverable amount is estimated. A previously recognised                         跡象，則對該可收回金額作出估計。之
    impairment loss of an asset other than goodwill is reversed only if there                    前已確認之資產（商譽除外）減值虧損僅
    has been a change in the estimates used to determine the recoverable                         會於用以釐定資產可收回金額之估計改
    amount of that asset, but not to an amount higher than the carrying                          變時撥回，惟撥回後之金額不得高於假
    amount that would have been determined (net of any depreciation/                             設過往年度並無就資產確認減值虧損而
    amortisation) had no impairment loss been recognised for the asset in                        應予以釐定之賬面值（扣除任何折舊╱攤
    prior years. A reversal of such an impairment loss is credited to profit                     銷）。減 值 虧 損 之 撥 回 於 產 生 期 間 計 入
    or loss in the period in which it arises, unless the asset is carried at a                   損益，惟若資產按重估金額入賬，則撥
    revalued amount, in which case the reversal of the impairment loss is                        回之減值虧損按該重估資產之相關會計
    accounted for in accordance with the relevant accounting policy for that                     政策列賬。
    revalued asset.

    parts as individual assets with specific useful lives and depreciates them                      作出折舊。
    accordingly.

    Valuations are performed frequently enough to ensure that the fair value                        估值會頻密進行，頻率足以確保重估資
    of a revalued asset does not differ materially from its carrying amount.                        產的公允值不會大幅偏離其賬面值。物

    to the statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income to                            均會計入損益及其他全面收益表，惟以
    the extent of the deficit previously charged. An annual transfer from the                       先前扣除的虧絀數額為限。每年由資產
    asset revaluation reserve to retained profits is made for the difference                        重估儲備轉撥至保留溢利乃就根據資產

    Leases                                                                                          租賃
    The Group assesses at contract inception whether a contract is, or                              本集團於合約開始時評估合約是否為或
    contains, a lease. A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys                  包含租賃。倘合約為換取代價而給予在
    the right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in                     一段時間內控制已識別資產使用的權
    exchange for consideration.                                                                     利，則該合約為或包含租賃。

    Group as a lessee                                                                               本集團作為承租人
    The Group applies a single recognition and measurement approach for                             本集團對所有租賃應用單一確認及計量
    all leases, except for short-term leases and leases of low-value assets. The                    方法，惟短期租賃及低價值資產租賃除
    Group recognises lease liabilities to make lease payments and right-of-                         外。本集團確認租賃負債以作出租賃付
    use assets representing the right to use the underlying assets.                                 款，而使用權資產指使用相關資產的權
                                                                                                    利。

    (a)   Right-of-use assets                                                                      (a)     使用權資產
          Right-of-use assets are recognised at the commencement date of                                    使用權資產於租賃開始日期（即相
          the lease (that is the date the underlying asset is available for use).                           關資產可供使用的日期）確認。使
          Right-of-use assets are measured at cost, less any accumulated                                    用權資產按成本減任何累計折舊
          depreciation and any impairment losses, and adjusted for any                                      及 任 何 減 值 虧 損 計 量，並 就 租 賃
          remeasurement of lease liabilities. The cost of right-of-use                                      負 債 之 任 何 重 新 計 量 作 出 調 整。
          assets includes the amount of lease liabilities recognised, initial                               使用權資產之成本包括已確認租
          direct costs incurred, and lease payments made at or before the                                   賃 負 債 金 額、已 發 生 初 始 直 接 成
          commencement date less any lease incentives received. Right-of-                                   本及於開始日期或之前支付之租
          use assets are depreciated on a straight-line basis over the shorter                              賃付款減已收取之任何租賃優惠。
          of the lease terms and the estimated useful lives of the assets as                                使用權資產於租賃期及估計使用

    Investments and other financial assets                                                投資及其他金融資產
    Initial recognition and measurement                                                   初步確認及計量
    Financial assets are classified, at initial recognition, as subsequently              金融資產於初始確認時分類為其後按攤
    measured at amortised cost, fair value through other comprehensive                    銷成本、按公允值計入其他全面收益及
    income, and fair value through profit or loss.                                        按公允值計入損益計量。

    The classification of financial assets at initial recognition depends on the          於初始確認時，金融資產分類取決於金
    financial asset’s contractual cash flow characteristics and the Group’s               融資產的合約現金流量特點及本集團管
    business model for managing them. With the exception of trade                         理該等金融資產的業務模式。除並無重
    receivables that do not contain a significant financing component or for              大融資成分或本集團已應用並未就重大
    which the Group has applied the practical expedient of not adjusting the              融資成分的影響作出調整的可行權宜方
    effect of a significant financing component, the Group initially measures             法的貿易應收款項外，本集團初步按公
    a financial asset at its fair value, plus in the case of a financial asset not at     允值加上（倘金融資產並非按公允值計
    fair value through profit or loss, transaction costs. Trade receivables that          入 損 益）交 易 成 本 計 量 金 融 資 產。並 無
    do not contain a significant financing component or for which the Group               重大融資成分或本集團已應用可行權宜
    has applied the practical expedient are measured at the transaction price             方法的貿易應收款項乃根據下文「收入
    determined under HKFRS 15 in accordance with the policies set out for                 確認」所載政策按香港財務報告準則第
    “Revenue recognition” below.                                                          15 號釐定的交易價格計量。

    In order for a financial asset to be classified and measured at amortised             為使金融資產按攤銷成本或按公允值
    cost or fair value through other comprehensive income, it needs to                    計入其他全面收益進行分類及計量，需
    give rise to cash flows that are solely payments of principal and interest            產生純粹為支付本金及未償還本金利
    (“SPPI”) on the principal amount outstanding. Financial assets with cash              息（「純粹為支付本金及利息」）的現金流
    flows that are not SPPI are classified and measured at fair value through             量。現金流量並非純粹為支付本金及利
    profit or loss, irrespective of the business model.                                   息的金融資產不論業務模式按公允值計
                                                                                          入損益計量。

    The Group’s business model for managing financial assets refers to                    本集團管理金融資產的業務模式指其
    how it manages its financial assets in order to generate cash flows.                  如何管理其金融資產以產生現金流量。
    The business model determines whether cash flows will result from                     業務模式確定現金流量是否來自收取合
    collecting contractual cash flows, selling the financial assets, or both.             約現金流量、出售金融資產，或兩者兼
    Financial assets classified and measured at amortised cost are held                   有。按攤銷成本分類及計量的金融資產
    within a business model with the objective to hold financial assets in                於持有金融資產的目的為收取合約現金
    order to collect contractual cash flows, while financial assets classified            流量的商業模式內持有，而按公允值計
    and measured at fair value through other comprehensive income are                     入其他全面收益分類及計量的金融資產
    held within a business model with the objective of both holding to                    於持有金融資產的目的為同時收取合約
    collect contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which are                現金流量及出售的商業模式內持有。並
    not held within the aforementioned business models are classified and                 非於上述商業模式內持有的金融資產按
    measured at fair value through profit or loss.                                        公允值計入損益分類及計量。

    Purchases or sales of financial assets that require delivery of assets within         購買或出售金融資產須於監管或市場慣
    the period generally established by regulation or convention in the                   例規定的期間內交付資產，則於交易日
    marketplace are recognised on the trade date, that is, the date that the             （即本集團承諾購買或出售資產的日期）
    Group commits to purchase or sell the asset.                                          確認。

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                                         2.4 重大會計政策（續）
    Impairment of financial assets                                                     金融資產減值
    The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit losses (“ECLs”)              本集團確認對並非按公允值計入損益的
    for all debt instruments not held at fair value through profit or loss. ECLs       所有債務工具預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸
    are based on the difference between the contractual cash flows due                 虧損」）的撥備。預期信貸虧損乃基於根
    in accordance with the contract and all the cash flows that the Group              據合約到期的合約現金流量與本集團預
    expects to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original                 期收取的所有現金流量之間的差額而釐
    effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will include cash flows           定、以原實際利率的近似值貼現。預期
    from the sale of collateral held or other credit enhancements that are             現金流量將包括出售所持抵押的現金流
    integral to the contractual terms.                                                 量或組成合約條款的其他信貸提升措施。

    General approach                                                                   一般方法
    ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures for which there            預期信貸虧損分兩個階段進行確認。就
    has not been a significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition,      自初始確認起信貸風險並無大幅增加的
    ECLs are provided for credit losses that result from default events that           信貸敞口而言，會就未來 12 個月內可能
    are possible within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those                 發生違約事件而導致的信貸虧損（12 個
    credit exposures for which there has been a significant increase in credit         月預期信貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損。
    risk since initial recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit losses     就自初始確認起信貸風險大幅增加的該
    expected over the remaining life of the exposure, irrespective of the              等信貸敞口而言，不論何時發生違約，
    timing of the default (a lifetime ECL).                                            於敞口的餘下年期內的預期信貸虧損均
                                                                                       須計提虧損撥備（全期預期信貸虧損）。

    At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether the credit risk on a            於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工具信
    financial instrument has increased significantly since initial recognition.        貸風險是否自初始確認起大幅增加。於
    When making the assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default               進行評估時，本集團比較金融工具於報
    occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting date with the            告日期發生違約的風險及金融工具於初
    risk of a default occurring on the financial instrument as at the date of          始確認日期發生違約的風險，認為在毋
    initial recognition and considers reasonable and supportable information           須付出過多成本或努力的情況下即可獲
    that is available without undue cost or effort, including historical and           得合理可靠的資料（包括歷史及前瞻性
    forward-looking information. The Group considers that there has been               資 料）。本 集 團 認 為 當 合 約 付 款 逾 期 30
    a significant increase in credit risk when contractual payments are more           天以上時，信貸風險會大幅增加。
    than 30 days past due.

    The Group considers a financial asset in default when contractual                  本 集 團 會 在 合 約 付 款 逾 期 90 日 時 考 慮
    payments are 90 days past due. However, in certain cases, the Group                金融資產違約。然而，在若干情況下，
    may also consider a financial asset to be in default when internal or              當內部或外部資料顯示，在並無計及本
    external information indicates that the Group is unlikely to receive the           集團持有的任何信貸提升措施情況時，
    outstanding contractual amounts in full before taking into account any             本集團不大可能悉數收到未償還合約款
    credit enhancements held by the Group. A financial asset is written off            項，則本集團亦可認為金融資產違約。
    when there is no reasonable expectation of recovering the contractual              倘無法合理預期收回合約現金流量，則
    cash flows.                                                                        撇銷金融資產。

    For trade receivables and contract assets that do not contain a significant                          就並無重大融資成分的貿易應收款項及

    Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and other payables,                                   按攤銷成本列賬的金融負債（貿易及其
    and borrowings)                                                                                      他應付款項以及借款）

2.4 MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                                         2.4 重大會計政策（續）
    Derecognition of financial liabilities                                             終止確認金融負債
    A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under the                當負債項下的責任被解除或取消或到期
    liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                                  時，則終止確認金融負債。

    When an existing financial liability is replaced by another from the same          倘現有金融負債由同一貸方按條款迥異
    lender on substantially different terms, or the terms of an existing liability     的其他負債取代，或現有金融負債的條
    are substantially modified, such an exchange or modification is treated            款經重大修訂，則該等變更或修訂被視
    as a derecognition of the original liability and a recognition of a new            作終止確認原有負債並確認新負債，各
    liability, and the difference between the respective carrying amounts is           賬面值的差額於損益及其他全面收益表
    recognised in the statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive              確認。
    income.

    Offsetting of financial instruments                                                抵銷金融工具
    Financial assets and financial liabilities are offset and the net amount           倘現時存在一項可依法強制執行的權利
    is reported in the statement of financial position if there is a currently         可抵銷已確認金額，且有意以淨額結算
    enforceable legal right to offset the recognised amounts and there is an           或同時變現資產及償付債務，則金融資
    intention to settle on a net basis, or to realise the assets and settle the        產及金融負債均可予抵銷，並將淨金額
    liabilities simultaneously.                                                        列入財務狀況表內。

    Inventories                                                                        存貨
    Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost         存貨按成本與可變現淨值兩者之較低者
    is determined by the weighted average method. Cost of finished goods               入賬。成本以加權平均法釐定，製成品
    comprises direct materials, direct labour and an appropriate proportion            成本包括直接物料成本、直接勞工成本
    of overheads. Net realisable value is based on estimated selling prices            及適當比例的間接成本。可變現淨值按
    less any estimated costs to be incurred to completion and disposal.                估計售價減完成及出售所需的任何估計
                                                                                       成本釐定。

    Cash and cash equivalents                                                          現金及現金等價物
    Cash and cash equivalents in the statement of financial position                   財務狀況表中的現金及現金等價物包括
    comprise cash on hand and at banks, and short-term highly liquid                   手頭現金及銀行現金，以及到期日通常
    deposits with a maturity of generally within three months that are readily         在三個月內的短期高流動性存款，其可
    convertible into known amounts of cash, subject to an insignificant risk           隨時轉換為已知金額的現金，價值變動
    of changes in value and held for the purpose of meeting short-term cash            風險很小及為滿足短期現金承擔而持有。
    commitments.

    For the purpose of the consolidated statement of cash flows, cash and              就綜合現金流量表而言，現金及現金等
    cash equivalents comprise cash on hand and at banks, and short-term                價物包括手頭現金及銀行存款以及短期
    deposits as defined above, less bank overdrafts which are repayable on             存款（定義見下文），不包括須於要求時
    demand and form an integral part of the Group’s cash management.                   償還的銀行透支）及屬本集團現金管理
                                                                                       主要組成部分。

    Provisions                                                                                    撥備
    A provision is recognised when a present obligation (legal or                                 倘因過往事件導致現有債務（法定或推

    a future outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation,                      債務，則確認撥備，惟須可靠估計有關
    provided that a reliable estimate can be made of the amount of the                            債務金額。
    obligation.

    When the effect of discounting is material, the amount recognised for a                       倘貼現的影響屬重大，則確認的撥備金
    provision is the present value at the end of the reporting period of the                      額為預期需用作償還債務的未來支出於
    future expenditures expected to be required to settle the obligation.                         各報告期末的現值。因時間推移而產生
    The increase in the discounted present value amount arising from the                          的貼現現值增額，計入損益表內的融資
    passage of time is included in finance costs in the statement of profit or                    成本。
    loss.

    the end of the reporting period, taking into consideration interpretations                    行詮釋及慣例，按預期自稅務機關退回
    and practices prevailing in the countries in which the Group operates.                        或付予稅務當局的金額計算。

          of goodwill or an asset or liability in a transaction that is not a                             務合併的交易中初步確認商譽或
          business combination and, at the time of the transaction, affects                               資 產 或 負 債 而 產 生，並 於 交 易 時

          does not give rise to equal taxable and deductible temporary                                    或虧損並不產生同等應納稅及可
          differences; and                                                                                抵扣暫時性差異；及

    expected to be settled or recovered.                                                  延稅項資產與遞延稅項負債可予抵銷。

    Government grants                                                                           政府補助金
    Government grants are recognised at their fair value where there is                         倘有合理保證將可獲得政府補助金，且
    reasonable assurance that the grant will be received and all attaching                      符合所有附帶條件，則補助金可按公允

    item, it is recognised as income on a systematic basis over the periods                     在必須將補助金於擬補償的成本支出期
    that the costs, for which it is intended to compensate, are expensed.                       間有系統地確認為收入。

    Revenue recognition                                                                         收益確認
    Revenue from contracts with customers                                                       來自客戶合約之收益
    Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised when control                            於貨品或服務的控制權按反映本集團預
    of goods or services is transferred to the customers at an amount that                      期就交換該等貨品或服務有權收取的代
    reflects the consideration to which the Group expects to be entitled in                     價的金額轉移予客戶時確認客戶合約收
    exchange for those goods or services.                                                       益。

    When the consideration in a contract includes a variable amount, the                        當合約代價包含可變金額時，代價金額
    amount of consideration is estimated to which the Group will be entitled                    估計為本集團就向客戶轉讓貨品或服務
    in exchange for transferring the goods or services to the customer. The                     而有權在交換中獲取的數額。可變代價
    variable consideration is estimated at contract inception and constrained                   於合約開始時估計並受到約束，直至其
    until it is highly probable that a significant revenue reversal in the                      後關乎可變代價的不確定因素獲得解決
    amount of cumulative revenue recognised will not occur when the                             時確認累計收益金額不大可能發生重大
    associated uncertainty with the variable consideration is subsequently                      收益撥回。
    resolved.

    Sale of nutritional supplements and packaged health food products                          銷售營養膳食補充劑及包裝保健食品
    Revenue from the sale of nutritional supplements and packaged                               銷售營養膳食補充劑及包裝保健食品的
    health food products is recognised at the point in time when control                        收益於資產控制權轉移予客戶時確認，
    of the asset is transferred to the customer, generally on delivery of the                   一般於交付營養膳食補充劑及包裝保健
    nutritional supplements and packaged health food products.                                  食品時確認。

    Some contracts provide customers with rights of return and volume                           若干合約向客戶提供退貨權及銷量回扣
    rebates, giving rise to variable consideration.                                             可引致可變代價。

    Other income                                                                    其他收入

    the dividend can be measured reliably.                                          量。

    Contract liabilities                                                                          合約負債
    A contract liability is recognised when a payment is received or a                            倘本集團在轉讓相關貨品或服務前自
    payment is due (whichever is earlier) from a customer before the Group                        客戶收取付款或付款到期（以較早者為
    transfers the related goods or services. Contract liabilities are recognised                  準），則 確 認 合 約 負 債。當 本 集 團 履 行
    as revenue when the Group performs under the contract (i.e., transfers                        合約（即將相關貨品或服務控制權轉讓
    control of the related goods or services to the customer).                                    予客戶）時，合約負債乃確認為收益。

    Right-of-return assets                                                                        退貨權資產
    A right-of-return asset is recognised for the right to recover the goods                      就有權收回預期客戶將退還的貨品確認
    expected to be returned by customers. The asset is measured at the                            退貨權資產。資產按將退還貨品原賬面
    former carrying amount of the goods to be returned, less any expected                         值減預期收回貨品的任何成本與退還貨
    costs to recover the goods and any potential decreases in the value of                        品價值的任何潛在減幅計量。本集團就
    the returned goods. The Group updates the measurement of the asset                            修訂預期退貨水平及退還貨品價值的任
    for any revisions to the expected level of returns and any additional                         何額外減幅更新資產的計量。
    decreases in the value of the returned goods.

    Refund liabilities                                                                            退款負債
    A refund liability is recognised for the obligation to refund some or                         就 退 還 自 客 戶 收 取（或 應 收）之 部 分 或
    all of the consideration received (or receivable) from a customer and                         全部代價的義務確認退款負債，並按本
    is measured at the amount the Group ultimately expects it will have                           集團最終預期其須退回予客戶的金額計
    to return to the customer. The Group updates its estimates of refund                          量。本集團於各報告期末更新其對退款
    liabilities (and the corresponding change in the transaction price) at the                    負債之估計（及交易價格之相應變動）。
    end of each reporting period.

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS AND                                               3.       重要會計判斷及估計
     ESTIMATES
     Judgements                                                                                   判斷
     In the process of applying the Group’s accounting policies, management                       應用本集團的會計政策時，除涉及估計
     has made the following judgements, apart from those involving                                者外，管理層已作出以下對財務報表已
     estimations, which have the most significant effect on the amounts                           確認金額影響最大的判斷：
     recognised in the financial statements:

     Operating lease commitments — Group as lessor                                                經營租賃承擔 — 本集團作為出租人

     Deferred tax assets                                                                           遞延稅項資產
     Deferred tax assets are recognised for unused tax losses to the extent                        倘可能有應課稅利潤可用以抵銷未動用
     that it is probable that taxable profit will be available against which the                   的稅務虧損，則就該等虧損確認遞延稅
     losses can be utilised. Significant management judgement is required                          項資產。管理層須根據未來應課稅利潤
     to determine the amount of deferred tax assets that can be recognised,                        的大致時間及數額以及未來稅務計劃策
     based upon the likely timing and the level of future taxable profits,                         略作出重大判斷，以釐定可確認的遞延
     together with future tax planning strategies.                                                 稅項資產金額。

     elsewhere in the Group. The subsidiaries have neither any taxable                             的附屬公司有關，尚未到期，且不可用
     temporary difference nor any tax planning opportunities available that                        作抵銷本集團其他部分的應課稅收入。
     could partly support the recognition of these losses as deferred tax                          該等附屬公司既無任何應課稅暫時差

27. RESERVES                                                                      27. 儲備
    The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements therein for the           本集團於本年度及過往年度的儲備金額
    current and prior years are presented in the consolidated statement of          及其變動於財務報表第 167 至 168 頁的綜
    changes in equity on pages 167 to 168 of the financial statements.              合權益變動表呈報。

    (i)    Statutory surplus reserve                                                (i)    法定盈餘儲備
           In accordance with the PRC regulations, certain companies in                    根 據 中 國 法 規，中 國 境 內 若 干 公
           the PRC are required to transfer part of their profits after tax                司 須 在 作 出 溢 利 分 派 前，將 其 部
           determined under the PRC accounting standards to the statutory                  分根據中國會計準則釐定的除稅
           surplus reserve fund, before profit distributions are made. The                 後 溢 利 轉 撥 至 法 定 盈 餘 儲 備 金。
           statutory surplus reserve fund is non-distributable and may be                  法 定 盈 餘 儲 備 金 不 可 分 派，可 用
           used either to offset losses, or for capitalisation issues by way of            於抵銷虧損或以實繳股本形式作
           paid-up capital.                                                                出資本化發行。

    (ii)   Capital reserve                                                          (ii)   資本儲備
           The amounts represented the balance of the credit amount arising                相關金額指實繳股本供款超出股
           from the excess of the par value of the shares from the paid-in                 份面值的進賬金額結餘。
           capital contribution.

    (iii) Other reserve                                                             (iii) 其他儲備
           This resulted from the acquisition of non-controlling shareholder.              因收購非控股股東而產生。

           fair value.                                                                     投資物業而產生。
